	create_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,
	handover_flag varchar(1) DEFAULT 'N'::character varying NULL, -- 交接标识
	warehouse_code varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 仓库代码
	inner_box_no varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 内袋号
	box_no varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 彩盒
	carton_no varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 外箱

	doc_no varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 单据号
	doc_category_code varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 单据类别
	CONSTRAINT t_wms_doc_upn_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_t_wms_doc_upn_3map ON public.t_wms_doc_upn USING btree (doc_id);

CREATE INDEX idx_t_wms_doc_upn_r3fv ON public.t_wms_doc_upn USING btree (item_id);

-- public.t_wms_downshelf_task definition

-- Drop table

-- DROP TABLE public.t_wms_downshelf_task;

CREATE TABLE public.t_wms_downshelf_task (
	id varchar(40) NOT NULL,
	task_code varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 任务代码
	task_type int4 DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, -- 任务类型
	pkg_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 任务id
	upn varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- upn
	qty numeric(16, 10) DEFAULT '0'::numeric NOT NULL, -- 数量
	shelf_no varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 货架号
	addr int4 NULL, -- 储位地址
	current_status int4 DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, -- 状态
	msg varchar(2000) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 消息
	task_param varchar(200) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 任务参数
	create_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL,
	create_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,

	fid varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- ERP主表ID
	fentryid varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- ERP子表ID
	need_fid varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 用料清单ID
	need_frowid varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 用料清单行ID
	remark varchar(2000) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 备注
	create_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL,
	create_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,
	update_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL,
	update_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,
	current_status int4 NULL, -- 当前状态(0,创建;1,开始;2,完结;3,删除)
	total_qty numeric(16, 10) DEFAULT NULL::numeric NULL, -- 总量(收料数量)
	out_qty numeric(16, 10) DEFAULT '0'::numeric NULL, -- 已出数量
	upload_qty numeric(16, 10) DEFAULT '0'::numeric NULL, -- 上传数量
	handover_qty numeric(16, 10) DEFAULT '0'::numeric NULL, -- 交接数量
	CONSTRAINT t_wms_outsourcing_pick_bill_detail_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_t_wms_outsourcing_pick_bill_detail_8qc2 ON public.t_wms_outsourcing_pick_bill_detail USING btree (doc_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_t_wms_outsourcing_pick_bill_detail_df3l ON public.t_wms_outsourcing_pick_bill_detail USING btree (part_id);
CREATE INDEX "index_T_WMS_OUTSOURCING_PICK_BILL_DETAIL_CREATE_TIME" ON public.t_wms_outsourcing_pick_bill_detail USING btree (create_time);

-- public.t_wms_pick_analyse_split_upn definition

-- Drop table

-- DROP TABLE public.t_wms_pick_analyse_split_upn;

CREATE TABLE public.t_wms_pick_analyse_split_upn (
	id varchar(40) NOT NULL,
	analyse_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 分析ID
	doc_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 源叫料单ID
	item_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 源叫料单细项
	machine_code varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 设备
	table_no varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 区域
	slot_no int4 NULL, -- 站位
	track_no varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 通道
	doc_id_to varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 目的叫料单ID
	item_id_to varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 目的叫料单细项ID
	machine_id_to varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 拆分到设备
	slot_id_to int4 NULL, -- 拆分到站位
	table_id_to varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 拆分到区域
	track_id_to varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 拆分到通道
	part_no varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- PART_NO
	part_level varchar(1) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 料号等级
	upn varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 物料唯一码
	qty int4 DEFAULT 0 NULL, -- 拆分前数量
	date_code varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 日期码
	lot_code varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 批号
	supplier_code varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 供应商代码
	split_qty int4 DEFAULT 0 NULL, -- 待拆分数量
	location_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 储位ID
	eshelf_type int4 NULL, -- 货架类型
	real_split_qty int4 DEFAULT 0 NULL, -- 实际拆分数量
	current_status int4 DEFAULT 0 NULL, -- 0,创建;1,取料;2,拆分；SplitStatus，取数据字典[LC-01-0042]
	warehouse_code varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 仓库
	item_seq int4 DEFAULT 0 NULL, -- 细项序号
	item_seq_to int4 DEFAULT 0 NULL, -- 拆分到细项序号
	customer_code varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 客户代码
	ma_code varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 制造商代码
	origin_place varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 原产地
	vendor_barcode varchar(200) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 供应商条码
	part_name varchar(200) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 品名
	part_spec varchar(200) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 规格
	rd_flag varchar(1) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 研发物料标志
	tray_flag varchar(1) DEFAULT 'N'::character varying NULL, -- 是否为托盘;取数据字典
	tray_flag_to varchar(1) DEFAULT 'N'::character varying NULL, -- 拆分到是否为托盘;取数据字典
	batch_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 每次分析后批量插入的ID
	last_flag varchar(1) DEFAULT 'N'::character varying NULL, -- 最后一盘;取数据字典
	create_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL,
	create_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,
	update_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL,
	update_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,
	CONSTRAINT t_wms_pick_analyse_split_upn_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_t_wms_pick_analyse_split_upn_3tp7 ON public.t_wms_pick_analyse_split_upn USING btree (upn);

	shelf_no varchar(40) NULL, -- 货架编码
	shelf_desc varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 描述
	warehouse_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 仓库
	shelf_type int4 NULL, -- 货架类型(0,普通货架;1,电子货架;2,感应货架;3,料塔;4,改造货架)
	ip varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- IP
	port int4 NULL, -- Port
	mac varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- MAC地址
	dns varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- DNS
	create_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL,
	create_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,
	update_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL,
	update_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,
	is_enabled varchar(1) DEFAULT 'Y'::character varying NULL,
	CONSTRAINT t_wms_shelf_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);

-- Column comments

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_shelf.shelf_no IS '货架编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_shelf.shelf_desc IS '描述';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_shelf.warehouse_id IS '仓库';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_shelf.shelf_type IS '货架类型(0,普通货架;1,电子货架;2,感应货架;3,料塔;4,改造货架)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_shelf.ip IS 'IP';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_shelf.port IS 'Port';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_shelf.mac IS 'MAC地址';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_shelf.dns IS 'DNS';

-- Drop table

-- DROP TABLE public.t_wms_shipping_bill;

CREATE TABLE public.t_wms_shipping_bill (
	id varchar(40) NOT NULL,
	doc_no varchar(40) NOT NULL, -- 单据编号
	customer_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 客户ID
	src_doc varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 源单据
	upload_flag varchar(1) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 上传ERP
	upload_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL, -- ERP上传时间
	upload_userid varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- ERP上传人员
	shipping_confirm varchar(1) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 出货确认
	upload_result varchar(200) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 过账结果
	fid varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- ERP主表ID
	current_status int4 NULL, -- 当前状态(0,创建;1,开始;2,完结;)
	doc_from varchar(2000) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 单据来源
	remark varchar(2000) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 备注
	create_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL,
	create_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,
	update_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL,
	update_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,
	doc_type_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 单据类型ID
	shipment_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL, -- 出货日期
	is_urgent varchar(1) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 加急标识
	CONSTRAINT t_wms_shipping_bill_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_t_wms_shipping_bill_doc_no ON public.t_wms_shipping_bill USING btree (doc_no);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_t_wms_shipping_bill_id ON public.t_wms_shipping_bill USING btree (id);
COMMENT ON TABLE public.t_wms_shipping_bill IS '销售出货单';

-- Column comments

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_shipping_bill.upload_flag IS '上传ERP';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_shipping_bill.upload_time IS 'ERP上传时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_shipping_bill.upload_userid IS 'ERP上传人员';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_shipping_bill.shipping_confirm IS '出货确认';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_shipping_bill.upload_result IS '过账结果';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_shipping_bill.fid IS 'ERP主表ID';

-- public.t_wms_stock definition

-- Drop table

-- DROP TABLE public.t_wms_stock;

CREATE TABLE public.t_wms_stock (
	id varchar(40) NOT NULL,
	upn varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 物料编号（最小包装号）
	receive_qty numeric(16, 10) DEFAULT NULL::numeric NULL, -- 接收数量（第一次入库）
	qty numeric(16, 10) DEFAULT NULL::numeric NULL, -- 当前数量
	supplier_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 供应商代码
	customer_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 客户代码
	date_code varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 生产日期
	lot_no varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 批次
	work_order varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 工单
	qc_result int4 NULL, -- QC结果
	current_status int4 DEFAULT 0 NULL, -- 当前状态（0,正常;1,不良;2,报废;）
	location_no varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 储位
	stock_status int4 DEFAULT 0 NULL, -- 库存状态(0,收料;1,在库;2,不在库;3,待交接)
	pick_doc varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 拣料单号
	pick_lock varchar(1) DEFAULT 'N'::character varying NULL, -- 拣料锁定 :Y-锁定；N-未锁定
	take_stock_status int4 DEFAULT 0 NULL, -- 盘点状态(0-未盘点;1-动态盘点(可以发料,出库需要删除盘点UPN);2-静态盘点)
	warehouse_code varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 仓库
	check_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL, -- 超期复检日期
	limit_day int4 NULL, -- 检验周期-有效期限(当超期复检不为空，则查询此期限)
	inventory_type int4 DEFAULT 0 NULL, -- 库存种类
	lcr_flag varchar(40) DEFAULT 'N'::character varying NULL, -- LRC测试标志
	vendor_barcode varchar NULL, -- 供应商条码
	expire_flag varchar(1) DEFAULT 'N'::character varying NULL, -- 超期标志
	bin_code varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- bin值代码
	origin_lot varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 原厂批号
	origin_date_code varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 原厂D/C 日期码
	brand_code varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 品牌代码
	hold_flag varchar(1) DEFAULT 'N'::character varying NULL, -- 是否锁定
	inner_box_no varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 内袋号
	box_no varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 彩盒号
	carton_no varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 外箱号
	pallet_no varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 栈板号
	remark varchar(200) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 备注
	create_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL,
	create_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,
	update_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL,
	update_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,
	part_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 料号
	CONSTRAINT t_wms_stock_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_t_wms_stock_2y0t ON public.t_wms_stock USING btree (update_time);
CREATE INDEX idx_t_wms_stock_69tm ON public.t_wms_stock USING btree (stock_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_t_wms_stock_7329 ON public.t_wms_stock USING btree (location_no);
CREATE INDEX idx_t_wms_stock_m2lx ON public.t_wms_stock USING btree (create_time);
CREATE INDEX idx_t_wms_stock_nrj0 ON public.t_wms_stock USING btree (part_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_t_wms_stock_q7jh ON public.t_wms_stock USING btree (pick_doc);
CREATE INDEX idx_t_wms_stock_q9dw ON public.t_wms_stock USING btree (pick_lock);
CREATE INDEX idx_t_wms_stock_vwym ON public.t_wms_stock USING btree (carton_no);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_t_wms_stock_t9vs ON public.t_wms_stock USING btree (upn);
COMMENT ON TABLE public.t_wms_stock IS '物料信息表';

-- Column comments

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.upn IS '物料编号（最小包装号）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.receive_qty IS '接收数量（第一次入库）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.qty IS '当前数量';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.supplier_id IS '供应商代码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.customer_id IS '客户代码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.date_code IS '生产日期';

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.work_order IS '工单';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.qc_result IS 'QC结果';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.current_status IS '当前状态（0,正常;1,不良;2,报废;）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.location_no IS '储位';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.stock_status IS '库存状态(0,收料;1,在库;2,不在库;3,待交接)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.pick_doc IS '拣料单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.pick_lock IS '拣料锁定 :Y-锁定；N-未锁定';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.take_stock_status IS '盘点状态(0-未盘点;1-动态盘点(可以发料,出库需要删除盘点UPN);2-静态盘点)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.warehouse_code IS '仓库';

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.limit_day IS '检验周期-有效期限(当超期复检不为空，则查询此期限)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.inventory_type IS '库存种类';

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.vendor_barcode IS '供应商条码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.expire_flag IS '超期标志';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.bin_code IS 'bin值代码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.origin_lot IS '原厂批号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.origin_date_code IS '原厂D/C 日期码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.brand_code IS '品牌代码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.hold_flag IS '是否锁定';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.inner_box_no IS '内袋号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.t_wms_stock.box_no IS '彩盒号';

CREATE TABLE public.t_wms_take_stock_no (
	id varchar(40) NOT NULL,
	doc_no varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 盘点批号
	doc_type_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 单据类型id
	current_status int4 DEFAULT 0 NULL, -- 当前状态(0,创建;1,开始;2,结束;3,平账)
	upload_flag varchar(1) DEFAULT 'N'::character varying NULL, -- 上传ERP
	upload_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL, -- 上传时间
	upload_user varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 上传ERP人员
	erp_result varchar(2000) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- ERP返回结果
	take_stock_mode int4 DEFAULT 0 NULL, -- 盘点模式（1.明盘、2.暗盘）
	create_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL,
	create_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,
	update_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL,
	update_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,
	inventory_method varchar(1) DEFAULT '1'::character varying NOT NULL, -- 盘点方式（1.静态盘点，2.动态盘点）
	CONSTRAINT t_wms_take_stock_no_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);

-- Column comments

	id varchar(40) NOT NULL,
	doc_no varchar(40) NOT NULL, -- 单据号

	create_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL,
	create_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,
	update_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL,
	update_time timestamp(6) DEFAULT NULL::timestamp without time zone NULL,
	doc_type_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 单据类别
	CONSTRAINT t_wms_transfer_bill_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);

);

	upload_user_id varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL::character varying NULL, -- 上传人员

);
